namespace {
void load_modular_dialects(MlirDialectRegistry registry) {
  M::MO::loadDialects(*unwrap(registry));
}

MlirOperation next_operation(MlirOperation op) {
  mlir::Operation *x = unwrap(op);
  return wrap(x->getNextNode());
}

MlirOperation prev_operation(MlirOperation op) {
  mlir::Operation *x = unwrap(op);
  return wrap(x->getPrevNode());
}

MlirOperation last_operation(MlirBlock block) {
  mlir::Block *x = unwrap(block);

  if (x->empty())
    return wrap((mlir::Operation *)nullptr);
  return wrap(&x->back());
}

/// Initializes M::Context when no global context exists, then registers it on
/// the given MLIR context so loadContext() succeeds for graph compilation and
/// kernel analysis.
///
/// Reuses the existing global M::Context when present (e.g. after
/// InferenceSession) if Init::Options match; otherwise creates it via
/// Init::getOrCreateContext with cpuDevice options aligned with
/// Engine::Context::create's default session (see comments in-body).
static void _init_and_register_max_context(MlirContext mlirCtx) {
  M::Init::Options options;
  M::AsyncRT::CPUDeviceOptions rtOptions;
  // AsyncRT::CPUDeviceOptions defaults mainWillDonate to true; Engine's
  // getOrCreateCPUDeviceOptions forces false.
  rtOptions.mainWillDonate = false;
  options.withCPUDeviceOptions(rtOptions);
  // Engine::Config defaults isCrashLogEnabled() to false;
  // Engine::Context::create passes withForceDisableCrashReporting(!that).
  options.withForceDisableCrashReporting(true);

  auto ctxOr = M::Init::getOrCreateContext("max-graph", options);
  if (ctxOr.isError())
    throw std::runtime_error(ctxOr.takeError().get());
  M::ContextRef maxContext = ctxOr.takeValue();

  M::registerContext(*unwrap(mlirCtx), maxContext);
}

M::ArrayElementsAttr array_attr(M::Driver::Python::Buffer &buffer,
                                M::MO::TensorType &type) {
  // Create an ND array from data, taking the size and shape from the tensor
  // type argument.
  auto spec = buffer.getSpec();

  llvm::ArrayRef<uint8_t> bytesArray(llvm::bit_cast<uint8_t *>(buffer.data()),
                                     spec.getSizeInBytes());

  auto dims = llvm::SmallVector<int64_t>(spec.begin(), spec.end());
  auto shapedType =
      mlir::RankedTensorType::get(type.getIntDims(), type.getElementType());

  return M::ArrayElementsAttr::get({bytesArray.data(), bytesArray.size()},
                                   shapedType);
}

char *frame_traceback(void *frame_ptr) {
  // This is simply
  // `''.join(traceback.format_list(frame))`
  nb::gil_scoped_acquire acquire{};
  auto frame = nb::handle(reinterpret_cast<PyObject *>(frame_ptr));
  nb::object os = nb::module_::import_("traceback");
  nb::object formatStack = os.attr("format_list");
  auto empty = nb::str("");
  nb::object emptyJoin = empty.attr("join");

  auto outStr = nb::cast<std::string>(emptyJoin(formatStack(frame)));

  char *out = new char[outStr.size() + 1]{};
  // Copy to a string owned by the caller.
  outStr.copy(out, outStr.size());
  return out;
}

void frame_decref(void *frame_ptr) {
  // Destruction is always on the main thread controlled by python.
  // It will always have the gil.
  auto frame = nb::handle(reinterpret_cast<PyObject *>(frame_ptr));
  frame.dec_ref();
}

void frame_incref(void *frame_ptr) {
  // Destruction is always on the main thread controlled by python.
  // It will always have the gil.
  auto frame = nb::handle(reinterpret_cast<PyObject *>(frame_ptr));
  frame.inc_ref();
}

MlirLocation frame_loc(MlirContext ctx, nb::handle frame) {
  // The resulting location handles incrementing and decrementing the object's
  // reference count, as the object will be interned on the MLIR context.
  mlir::Location loc = M::RMO::PythonFrameAttr::getOpaque(
      unwrap(ctx), {.ptr = frame.ptr(),
                    .to_str = &frame_traceback,
                    .incref = &frame_incref,
                    .decref = &frame_decref});
  return wrap(loc);
}

// Serializes op and its descendants to MLIR bytecode with each op's
// Python-frame source location spelled out. The frames are attached as
// OpaqueLocs that hold a live callback and do not survive serialization, so
// they are materialized into serializable FusedLocs first. The materialization
// runs on a clone, so the caller's operation keeps its live OpaqueLocs.
//
// Bytecode rather than assembly text because this extension static-links its
// own LLVM: its textual float printer matches APFloat semantics by pointer
// against its own copy of the APFloatBase::sem* statics, so it mangles f64
// constants and aborts on any f32 that MLIR spells as a hex literal. Callers
// reparse this through max.mlir, which shares libmax's copy, and print there.
// See GEX-4052.
nb::bytes source_locations_bytecode(mlir::OpState &op) {
  mlir::OwningOpRef<mlir::Operation *> clone(op.getOperation()->clone());
  M::RMO::materializePythonFrameLocations(clone.get());
  std::string result;
  llvm::raw_string_ostream os(result);
  if (mlir::failed(mlir::writeBytecodeToFile(clone.get(), os)))
    throw std::runtime_error(
        "Failed to serialize operation with source locations to bytecode");
  return nb::bytes(result.data(), result.size());
}

/// Creates a Buffer directly from an MLIR constant attribute.
///
/// Supports:
/// - ArrayElementsAttr (#M.dense_array)
/// - AlignedBytesAttr (#M.aligned_bytes)
/// - DenseResourceElementsAttr (external blob)
///
/// Always copies data into a new buffer. Handles splatting when source
/// has 1 element but target shape requires more.
M::Driver::Python::Buffer
buffer_from_constant_attr(mlir::ElementsAttr attr, M::DType dtype,
                          const std::vector<size_t> &shape,
                          M::Driver::Python::PyDeviceTy device) {
  // Extract raw bytes based on attribute type
  llvm::ArrayRef<uint8_t> rawData;

  if (auto arrayAttr = llvm::dyn_cast<M::ArrayElementsAttr>(attr)) {
    rawData = arrayAttr.getRawData();
  } else if (auto alignedAttr = llvm::dyn_cast<M::AlignedBytesAttr>(attr)) {
    rawData = alignedAttr.getData();
  } else if (auto denseResource =
                 llvm::dyn_cast<mlir::DenseResourceElementsAttr>(attr)) {
    auto blob = denseResource.getRawHandle().getBlob();
    if (!blob)
      throw std::runtime_error("DenseResourceElementsAttr has no blob data");
    auto blobData = blob->getData();
    rawData = llvm::ArrayRef<uint8_t>(
        reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t *>(blobData.data()), blobData.size());
  } else {
    throw std::runtime_error(
        "Unsupported constant attribute type for buffer creation");
  }

  // Calculate element counts
  size_t targetNumElements = 1;
  for (size_t dim : shape)
    targetNumElements *= dim;

  size_t elementSize = dtype.getSizeInBytes();
  size_t srcNumElements = rawData.size() / elementSize;

  // Validate for splatting
  if (srcNumElements != targetNumElements && srcNumElements != 1) {
    throw std::runtime_error(
        "Invalid constant: source has " + std::to_string(srcNumElements) +
        " elements but target has " + std::to_string(targetNumElements) +
        " elements (not a splat)");
  }

  // TODO(MXF-106): use Buffer.frombuffer when available.
  // Create buffer and copy data
  M::Driver::Python::Buffer buffer(dtype, shape, device);
  uint8_t *dstData = static_cast<uint8_t *>(buffer.data());

  if (srcNumElements == targetNumElements) {
    // Direct copy
    std::memcpy(dstData, rawData.data(), rawData.size());
  } else {
    // Splat: replicate single element
    for (size_t i = 0; i < targetNumElements; ++i) {
      std::memcpy(dstData + i * elementSize, rawData.data(), elementSize);
    }
  }

  return buffer;
}

} // namespace

void M::Graph::Python::bindGraphModule(nb::module_ m) {
  m.doc() = R"nbdoc(
        Provides Python bindings for the MAX Graph API.
    )nbdoc";

  /// Attribute factories

  /// Register dialects needed to build MAX graphs.
  m.def("load_modular_dialects", &load_modular_dialects,
        "Registers all Modular MLIR dialects into the given registry.");

  /// Creates an array attribute from any Python object that implements the
  /// buffer protocol.
  /// Copies the buffer data into the array attribute interned into the
  /// MLIRContext.
  m.def("array_attr", &array_attr,
        "Creates an array attribute from a buffer and tensor type.");

  /// Creates a Buffer from an MLIR constant attribute (inverse of array_attr).
  /// Copies data and handles splatting.
  m.def("_buffer_from_constant_attr", &buffer_from_constant_attr,
        nb::arg("attr"), nb::arg("dtype"), nb::arg("shape"), nb::arg("device"),
        "Creates a Buffer from an MLIR constant attribute.");

  m.def("next_operation", &next_operation,
        "Returns the next operation in the parent block, or ``None``.");
  m.def("prev_operation", &prev_operation,
        "Returns the previous operation in the parent block, or ``None``.");
  m.def("last_operation", &last_operation,
        "Returns the last operation in the given block, or ``None``.");

  /// Converts a dtype to an MLIR type.
  m.def("dtype_to_type",
        infer_context(+[](mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, M::DType dtype) {
          auto mlirType = M::MO::toMLIRType(ctx, dtype);
          if (mlirType.isError())
            throw std::runtime_error("Invalid dtype");
          return *mlirType;
        }),
        "Converts a MAX :class:`DType` to the corresponding MLIR type.");

  m.def(
      "type_to_dtype",
      [](mlir::Type dtype) -> M::DType {
        ErrorOr<DType> type = M::MO::fromMLIRType(dtype);
        if (type.isError())
          throw std::runtime_error("Invalid type");
        return *type;
      },
      "Converts an MLIR type to the corresponding MAX :class:`DType`.");

  /// Creates an opaque location containing a python frame.
  m.def("frame_loc", &frame_loc,
        "Creates an opaque MLIR location containing a Python stack frame.");

  m.def("profile_scope_location", &profile_scope_location, "ctx"_a, "name"_a,
        "child"_a, "color"_a = std::nullopt,
        "Wraps `child` in a MOGG::ProfileScopeLocationAttr naming the active "
        "Graph.profile_scope(), with an optional color.");

  /// Serializes an operation to MLIR bytecode with Python source locations
  /// materialized, so a graph dumped without compiling it still shows where
  /// each op was built. Does not mutate the operation.
  m.def("source_locations_bytecode", &source_locations_bytecode, "op"_a,
        "Serializes the operation to MLIR bytecode with each op's Python "
        "source location materialized into printable form. Print it by "
        "reparsing through max.mlir. Does not mutate the operation.");

  m.def("_init_and_register_max_context", &_init_and_register_max_context,
        "mlir_ctx"_a,
        R"nbdoc(
      Initializes a process-wide M::Context when none exists, then registers it with the given MLIR context so compiler code can use loadContext.

      Internal API; does not expose M::Context to Python. New contexts are
      created via Init::getOrCreateContext (same Init path the Engine uses before
      attaching devices).
  )nbdoc");

  //===----------------------------------------------------------------------===//
  // KernelLibrary (backed by `mogg.kernel.decl` / `mogg.primitive.decl` ops)
  //===----------------------------------------------------------------------===//

  nb::class_<Python::KernelTorchInfo>(m, "KernelTorchInfo", R"nbdoc(
      The info needed to register a Mojo kernel as a PyTorch custom op.

      Built by :obj:`max.experimental.torch` from the kernel's ``execute``
      signature via ``KernelDeclAdaptor``.
  )nbdoc")
      .def_ro("num_dps_outputs", &Python::KernelTorchInfo::numDpsOutputs,
              "The number of leading destination-passing-style output "
              "arguments.")
      .def_ro("tensor_arg_names", &Python::KernelTorchInfo::tensorArgNames,
              "The source names of tensor-like arguments in declaration "
              "order (DPS outputs first, then inputs).");

  nb::class_<Python::Analysis>(m, "Analysis", R"nbdoc(
      Analyzes Mojo kernel libraries for custom operator integration.

      Loads one or more Mojo shared libraries and exposes their exported
      kernel operations for use in MAX graphs. Used internally to validate
      and register custom operations.

      Args:
          context: A Mojo library object or path.
          paths: The additional library search paths.
  )nbdoc")
      .def(nb::init<nb::object, std::vector<std::filesystem::path>>(),
           "context"_a, "paths"_a)
      .def_prop_ro("symbol_names", &Analysis::symbolNames,
                   "The list of exported kernel symbol names.")
      .def_prop_ro("library_paths", &Analysis::libraryPaths,
                   "The paths of all loaded libraries.")
      .def(
          "kernel",
          [](Analysis *self, const std::string &name) {
            return M::Graph::Python::castOp(self->kernelOp(name));
          },
          "Returns the MLIR operation for the named kernel symbol.")
      .def("verify_custom_op", &Analysis::verifyCustomOp,
           "Verifies that an operation matches the expected kernel signature.")
      .def("add_path", &Analysis::addPath,
           "Adds a library search path to this analysis.")
      .def("seed_kernel_decls", &Analysis::seedKernelDecls,
           "Copies kernel decl ops and the opaque-type mapping attribute "
           "into the given target module. Called by the compile path to "
           "seed a model module so the GC pipeline can skip "
           "``mogg-import-packages``.")
      .def("kernel_torch_info", &Analysis::kernelTorchInfo, "name"_a,
           "Returns the :class:`KernelTorchInfo` that "
           ":obj:`max.experimental.torch` needs to register the named kernel "
           "as a PyTorch custom op. Raises ``ValueError`` if the kernel is "
           "unknown or has an unsupported argument type.")
      .def("has_shape_function", &Analysis::hasShapeFunction, "name"_a,
           "Returns true if a shape function is registered for the named "
           "kernel.");

  M::Profiler::Python::addTracingToNanobindModuleClassMethods(
      m, "max._core.graph.");
}

MlirLocation profile_scope_location(MlirContext ctx, const std::string &name,
                                    MlirLocation child,
                                    std::optional<std::string> color) {
  mlir::MLIRContext *ctxPtr = unwrap(ctx);
  mlir::StringAttr colorAttr =
      color ? mlir::StringAttr::get(ctxPtr, *color) : mlir::StringAttr();
  mlir::Location loc = M::MOGG::ProfileScopeLocationAttr::get(
      ctxPtr, mlir::StringAttr::get(ctxPtr, name), unwrap(child), colorAttr);
  return wrap(loc);
}

#include "Init/Init.h"
#include "AsyncRT/Runtime/HostSystem.h"
#include "Init/DevelopmentSignalHandler.h"
#include "Support/Configuration.h"
#include "Support/ContextGlobal.h"
#include "Support/CrashReporting/CrashReporting.h"
#include "Support/Telemetry/Telemetry.h"

#include "llvm/Support/ErrorHandling.h"
#include "llvm/Support/Process.h"

#include <mutex>

using namespace M;

static constexpr bool isProductionBuild() {
#ifdef MODULAR_PRODUCTION
  return true;
#else
  return false;
#endif
}

namespace {

/// Internal function for creating an M::Context, not thread-safe, and not
/// intended for use outside of this file.
ErrorOr<ContextRef> createContextImpl(StringRef programName,
                                      const Init::Options &options,
                                      StringRef subCommand) {
  // Create the top-level context.
  ContextRef ctx = ContextRef::create();

  // Create the settings object.
  auto settingsOr = Config::open();
  if (settingsOr.isError())
    return settingsOr.takeError();
  Config settings = std::move(*settingsOr);

  bool crashReportingEnabled = Telemetry::isCrashReportingEnabled(settings);

  // Enable crash logging, if appropriate.
  if (!isProductionBuild() && !crashReportingEnabled)
    Init::registerDevelopmentSignalHandler(programName);
  else if (!options.forceDisableCrashReportingEnabled() &&
           crashReportingEnabled) {
    // The crash lane must carry the same machine/session IDs as the usage
    // telemetry lane so crash reports can be joined with usage events.
    const auto &localIDs = Telemetry::createLocalIDs();
    initCrashpadForProgram(programName, localIDs.machine,
                           localIDs.machineSource, localIDs.session, &settings);
  }

  // TelemetryContext created and added to the Context here as Config is
  // required.
  ctx->emplace<Telemetry::TelemetryContext>(settings, programName, subCommand);

  // Create a new cpuDevice (if needed).
  if (options.getCPUDeviceOptions()) {
    std::string profileFilename =
        llvm::sys::Process::GetEnv("MODULAR_PROFILE_FILENAME").value_or("");
    AsyncRT::CPUDeviceOptions opts = *options.getCPUDeviceOptions();
    if (!profileFilename.empty())
      opts.profileFilename = profileFilename;
    AsyncRT::CPUDeviceRef ref = AsyncRT::getOrCreateCPUDevice(
        AsyncRT::CPUDeviceSource::MaxContext, opts);
    ctx->setRuntime(GenericRCRef::fromRCRef(std::move(ref)));
  }

  // Finally move the settings.
  ctx->emplace<Config>(std::move(settings));

  // Store a copy of the init options so we can compare when reusing the
  // global context.
  ctx->emplace<Init::Options>(options);

  // Return the useable context.
  return std::move(ctx);
}

} // namespace

ErrorOr<ContextRef> Init::createContext(StringRef programName,
                                        const Init::Options &options,
                                        StringRef subCommand) {
  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(getGlobalContextMutex());
  if (getCurrentMaxContextPointerOrNull()) {
    llvm::report_fatal_error(
        "Init::createContext() attempted to create a new M::Context when one"
        "already exists, considering using Init::getContext() instead.");
  }

  // Create context.
  auto ctxOr = createContextImpl(programName, options, subCommand);
  if (ctxOr.isError())
    return ctxOr.takeError();

  // Set as the global current context.
  setCurrentMaxContextPointer(ctxOr->getPointer());
  return ctxOr;
}

ErrorOr<ContextRef> Init::getOrCreateContext(StringRef programName,
                                             const Init::Options &options,
                                             StringRef subCommand) {
  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(getGlobalContextMutex());
  if (Context *existing = getCurrentMaxContextPointerOrNull()) {
    if (*existing->get<Init::Options>() != options)
      llvm::report_fatal_error(
          "Init::getOrCreateContext() requested an M::Context with different"
          "Init::Options to those used to create the existing M::Context, check"
          "the options that are being requested.");
    return ContextRef::copy(existing);
  }

  // Create context.
  auto ctxOr = createContextImpl(programName, options, subCommand);
  if (ctxOr.isError())
    return ctxOr.takeError();

  // Set as the global current context.
  setCurrentMaxContextPointer(ctxOr->getPointer());
  return ctxOr;
}

ContextRef Init::getContext() {
  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(getGlobalContextMutex());
  Context *existing = getCurrentMaxContextPointerOrNull();
  if (!existing) {
    llvm::report_fatal_error(
        "Init::getContext() requested the M::Context but no context has been"
        "created yet, Init::createContext() must be called to create the"
        "M::Context before calling Init::getContext().");
  }

  return ContextRef::copy(existing);
}

#ifndef SUPPORT_CONTEXT_H
#define SUPPORT_CONTEXT_H

#include "Support/ADT/GenericRCRef.h"
#include "Support/ADT/GenericUniquePtrSet.h"
#include "Support/ErrorOr.h"
#include "Support/RCRef.h"
#include "Support/ReferenceCounted.h"
#include "Support/SymbolExport.h"
#include "Support/TypeID.h"
#include "llvm/ADT/FunctionExtras.h"
#include <memory>

namespace M {

class Context : public ReferenceCounted<Context> {
public:
  /// Transfers ptr into the context object set.
  template <typename T>
  void set(std::unique_ptr<T> ptr) {
    storage.set(std::move(ptr));
  }

  /// Sets the cpuDevice for this context, called at context creation. Uses
  /// GenericRCRef because of a circular dependency (Context -> AsyncRT/Runtime
  /// -> AsyncRT/Support -> MArchTarget -> MDialect -> Context). See GEX-3400.
  void setRuntime(GenericRCRef ref) { cpuDeviceRef = std::move(ref); }

  /// Emplaces a new object of type T into the context object set and returns a
  /// reference to it.
  template <typename T, typename... Args>
  T &emplace(Args &&...args) {
    return storage.emplace<T, Args...>(std::forward<Args>(args)...);
  }

  /// Returns a reference to the object of type T held by the context object
  /// set. If it does not contain such an object, emplaces a new object and
  /// returns a reference to it.
  template <typename T, typename... Args>
  T &emplaceIfMissing(Args &&...args) {
    return storage.emplaceIfMissing<T, Args...>(std::forward<Args>(args)...);
  }

  /// Returns a pointer to the object of type T held by the context object set.
  /// If it does not contain such an object, calls the creator function to
  /// create one and install. Returns any error the creator function returns.
  template <typename T>
  ErrorOr<T *> createIfMissing(
      llvm::unique_function<ErrorOr<std::unique_ptr<T>>()> creator) {
    return storage.createIfMissing<T>(std::move(creator));
  }

  /// Returns a pointer to the context object of type T held by the context
  /// object set, or nullptr if no such object exists. The CPUDeviceRef is
  /// stored as a separate member so is checked first.
  template <typename T>
  T *get() {
    if (cpuDeviceRef && cpuDeviceRef.getTypeID() == TypeID::get<T>())
      return cpuDeviceRef.get<T>();
    return storage.get<T>();
  }

  MODULAR_CXX_EXPORT ~Context();

private:
  GenericRCRef cpuDeviceRef;
  GenericUniquePtrSet storage;
};

/// Convenience definitions.
using ContextRef = RCRef<Context>;

} // namespace M

#endif // SUPPORT_CONTEXT_H

#ifndef SUPPORT_RCREF_H
#define SUPPORT_RCREF_H

#include <cassert>
#include <cstddef>
#include <type_traits>
#include <utility>

namespace M {

/// This is a smart pointer that keeps the specified reference counted value
/// around.  It is move-only to avoid accidental copies, but it can be copied
/// explicitly.
template <typename T>
class RCRef {
public:
  RCRef() : pointer(nullptr) {}
  RCRef(RCRef &&other) : pointer(other.pointer) { other.pointer = nullptr; }

  // See below; prefer the `copy` method.
  RCRef(const RCRef &other) : RCRef(std::move(other.copy())) {}

  /// This constructor forms a reference to the specified pointer, increasing
  /// the underlying reference count by 1.
  static RCRef copy(T *pointer) {
    if (pointer)
      pointer->addRef();
    return take(pointer);
  }

  /// This constructor forms a reference to the specified pointer, taking
  /// ownership it, and thus not increasing the reference count.
  static RCRef take(T *pointer) {
    RCRef<T> ref;
    ref.pointer = pointer;
    return ref;
  }

  /// Create an instance of T with the specified constructor arguments and
  /// return it as an RCRef.
  template <typename... Args>
  static RCRef create(Args &&...args) {
    return take(new T(std::forward<Args>(args)...));
  }

  /// Support implicit conversion from RCRef<Derived> to RCRef<Base>.
  template <typename U,
            typename = std::enable_if_t<std::is_base_of<T, U>::value>>
  RCRef(RCRef<U> &&u) : pointer(u.release()) {}

  ~RCRef() {
    if (pointer) {
#if defined(__GNUC__) && !defined(__clang__)
#pragma GCC diagnostic ignored "-Wmaybe-uninitialized"
#endif
      pointer->dropRef();
#if defined(__GNUC__) && !defined(__clang__)
#pragma GCC diagnostic pop
#endif
    }
  }

  RCRef &operator=(RCRef &&other) {
    if (pointer)
      pointer->dropRef();
    pointer = other.pointer;
    other.pointer = nullptr;
    return *this;
  }

  /// Manually drop the reference in this RCRef, setting it to null.
  void reset() {
    if (pointer)
      pointer->dropRef();
    pointer = nullptr;
  }

  /// Take ownership of the underlying pointer away from the RCRef and reset it
  /// to null.
  T *release() {
    T *tmp = pointer;
    pointer = nullptr;
    return tmp;
  }

  T &operator*() const {
    assert(pointer && "null RCRef");
    return *pointer;
  }

  T *operator->() const {
    assert(pointer && "null RCRef");
    return pointer;
  }

  /// Return a raw pointer.
  T *getPointer() const { return pointer; }

  void *getOpaquePointer() const { return reinterpret_cast<void *>(pointer); }

  /// Make an explicit copy of this RCRef, increasing the refcount by one.
  RCRef<T> copy() const { return RCRef<T>::copy(pointer); }

  /// Test for null.
  explicit operator bool() const { return pointer != nullptr; }

  void swap(RCRef &other) {
    using std::swap;
    swap(pointer, other.pointer);
  }

  /// Low level access for manipulating reference counts.  This allows the
  /// addRef/dropRef to be private on the classes themselves, forcing clients to
  /// go through RCRef.
  static void lowLevelAddRef(T *pointer) { pointer->addRef(); }
  static void lowLevelDropRef(T *pointer) { pointer->dropRef(); }
  static void lowLevelAddRef(T *pointer, size_t amount) {
    pointer->addRef(amount);
  }
  static void lowLevelDropRef(T *pointer, size_t amount) {
    pointer->dropRef(amount);
  }

private:
  // Per above, this type *is* implicitly copyable, as this is required for use
  // in certain contexts where the parent type is copied. However, this is
  // strongly discouraged, and we can disable at least the implicit copy
  // assignment operator. The `copy` method is strongly preferred.
  RCRef &operator=(const RCRef &) = delete;

  T *pointer;
};

// These global functions help make type inference work better.

/// Forms a reference to the specified pointer, increasing the underlying
/// reference count by 1.
template <typename T>
inline RCRef<T> copyRCRef(T *ptr) {
  return RCRef<T>::copy(ptr);
}

/// Form a reference to the specified pointer, taking ownership it, and thus not
/// increasing the reference count.
template <typename T>
inline RCRef<T> takeRCRef(T *ptr) {
  return RCRef<T>::take(ptr);
}

/// For ADL style swap.
template <typename T>
inline void swap(RCRef<T> &a, RCRef<T> &b) {
  a.swap(b);
}

} // namespace M

#endif // SUPPORT_RCREF_H

using namespace M;

//===---------------------------------------------------------------------===//
// MContextExtension
//===---------------------------------------------------------------------===//

namespace {

/// Dialect extension to inject an RCRef<M::Context> into MDialect.
class MContextExtension : public mlir::DialectExtensionBase {
public:
  MLIR_DEFINE_EXPLICIT_INTERNAL_INLINE_TYPE_ID(MContextExtension)

  /// Apply this extension once MDialect is loaded.
  explicit MContextExtension(ContextRef ref, bool enableThreadPool)
      : DialectExtensionBase(MDialect::getDialectNamespace()),
        ctxRef(std::move(ref)), enableThreadPool(enableThreadPool) {}

  /// Apply this extension to the given context and the required dialects.
  void apply(MLIRContext *context,
             MutableArrayRef<Dialect *> dialects) const final {
    auto *dialect = cast<MDialect>(dialects.front());

    dialect->setInternal(ctxRef.copy());
    if (context->isMultithreadingEnabled() || !enableThreadPool)
      return;
    AsyncRT::LLVMThreadPool *tp = ctxRef->get<AsyncRT::LLVMThreadPool>();
    if (!tp) {
      if (AsyncRT::CPUDevice *cpuDevice = ctxRef->get<AsyncRT::CPUDevice>())
        tp = &ctxRef->emplace<AsyncRT::LLVMThreadPool>(*cpuDevice);
    }

    // If the cpuDevice is available, enable threading in MLIR with it.
    if (tp)
      context->setThreadPool(*tp);
  }

  /// Return a copy of this extension.
  std::unique_ptr<DialectExtensionBase> clone() const final {
    return std::make_unique<MContextExtension>(ctxRef.copy(), enableThreadPool);
  }

private:
  ContextRef ctxRef;
  bool enableThreadPool;
};

} // namespace

void M::registerContext(mlir::DialectRegistry &registry, ContextRef &ref,
                        bool enableThreadPool) {
  std::unique_ptr<mlir::DialectExtensionBase> ctxExtension =
      std::make_unique<MContextExtension>(ref.copy(), enableThreadPool);
  registry.addExtension(mlir::TypeID::get<MContextExtension>(),
                        std::move(ctxExtension));
}

void M::registerContext(mlir::MLIRContext &ctx, ContextRef &ref,
                        bool enableThreadPool) {
  DialectRegistry registry;
  registerContext(registry, ref, enableThreadPool);
  ctx.appendDialectRegistry(registry);
}

ContextRef M::loadContext(mlir::MLIRContext *ctx) {
  StringRef name = MDialect::getDialectNamespace();
  return static_cast<MDialect *>(ctx->getOrLoadDialect(name))->getInternal();
}

from __future__ import annotations

import atexit
import contextlib
import functools
import inspect
from collections.abc import Callable, Generator
from concurrent.futures import Future, ThreadPoolExecutor
from typing import ParamSpec, TypeVar

from max import mlir

_DEFAULT_MLIR_CONTEXT = mlir.Context()
# MLIR Context.current is thread-local.
# - Keep the global context entered for the main thread.
# - Run worker tasks via helpers/executors in this module so MLIR APIs run
#   with a scoped context (avoids nanobind GIL/TLS teardown crashes).
# - atexit handler ensures the context is exited on shutdown.
_DEFAULT_MLIR_CONTEXT.__enter__()
atexit.register(_DEFAULT_MLIR_CONTEXT.__exit__, None, None, None)

P = ParamSpec("P")
R = TypeVar("R")


def default_mlir_context() -> mlir.Context:
    """Returns the global MLIR context."""
    return _DEFAULT_MLIR_CONTEXT


def call_with_default_mlir_context(
    fn: Callable[P, R], /, *args: P.args, **kwargs: P.kwargs
) -> R:
    context = mlir.Context.current
    if context is None:
        with _DEFAULT_MLIR_CONTEXT:
            return fn(*args, **kwargs)
    return fn(*args, **kwargs)


@contextlib.contextmanager
def ensure_default_mlir_context() -> Generator[None]:
    """Ensure the default MLIR context is entered for the current thread.

    Sister to :func:`call_with_default_mlir_context` for use as a context
    manager. ``mlir.Context.current`` is thread-local; background threads
    do not automatically have the default context entered. This enters it
    only if no context is already active.
    """
    if mlir.Context.current is None:
        with _DEFAULT_MLIR_CONTEXT:
            yield
    else:
        yield


def in_default_mlir_context(fn: Callable[P, R]) -> Callable[P, R]:
    """Decorator equivalent of ``with ensure_default_mlir_context():``. Sync + async."""
    if inspect.iscoroutinefunction(fn):

        @functools.wraps(fn)
        async def async_wrapper(*args: P.args, **kwargs: P.kwargs) -> R:
            with ensure_default_mlir_context():
                return await fn(*args, **kwargs)

        return async_wrapper  # type: ignore[return-value]

    @functools.wraps(fn)
    def wrapper(*args: P.args, **kwargs: P.kwargs) -> R:
        with ensure_default_mlir_context():
            return fn(*args, **kwargs)

    return wrapper


class MLIRThreadPoolExecutor(ThreadPoolExecutor):
    def submit(
        self,
        fn: Callable[P, R],
        /,
        *args: P.args,
        **kwargs: P.kwargs,
    ) -> Future[R]:
        return super().submit(
            call_with_default_mlir_context, fn, *args, **kwargs
        )

// Holds all the information necessary to execute and replace all constant
// subgraphs in a single graph op.
struct SubgraphsInfo {
  GraphOp orig;
  std::string constantSubgraphName;
  // Need the subgraph for its output values.
  SmallVector<Subgraph> subgraphs;
};

using RuntimeTensor = std::pair<ElementsAttr, TensorShape>;

struct ModelResult {
  /// Subgraph output op to its replacement constants, with runtime shapes so
  /// unknown dims can be resolved.
  llvm::DenseMap<Operation *, SmallVector<RuntimeTensor>> constants;
  /// Subgraph parameter name to its folded value.
  llvm::DenseMap<StringAttr, int64_t> parameters;
};

// Models need to ensure that their backing buffer lives as long as they do.
struct ModelsWithBuffer {
  ModelList models;
  BufferRef buffer;
};

} // namespace

static const Model *findModelByName(const ModelList &models, StringRef name) {
  for (const std::unique_ptr<const Model> &model : models) {
    if (model && model->getName() == name)
      return model.get();
  }
  return nullptr;
}

llvm::SetVector<StringAttr> M::MO::Subgraph::paramDeclarations() const {
  llvm::SetVector<StringAttr> params;
  for (auto op : ops) {
    op->walk([&](KGEN::ParamOpInterface paramOp) {
      paramOp.walkDeclarations(
          [&](KGEN::ParamDeclAttr decl) { params.insert(decl.getName()); });
      paramOp.walkDefinitions(
          [&](KGEN::ParamDeclAttr decl, const KGEN::ParamDefValue &val) {
            params.insert(decl.getName());
          });
    });
  }
  return params;
}

/// Topologically sort parameters in a list of ops.
static llvm::SmallVector<Operation *>
sortParams(llvm::ArrayRef<Operation *> ops) {
  // ParameterUseDefGraph doesn't map from parameter to operations using it, so
  // we have to construct our own.
  llvm::DenseMap<StringAttr, llvm::SetVector<StringAttr>> uses;
  llvm::DenseMap<StringAttr, Operation *> defs;
  for (auto op : ops) {
    op->walk([&](KGEN::ParamOpInterface paramOp) {
      paramOp.walkDeclarations([&](KGEN::ParamDeclAttr decl) {
        defs[decl.getName()] = op;
        uses[decl.getName()] = {};
      });
      paramOp.walkDefinitions(
          [&](KGEN::ParamDeclAttr decl, const KGEN::ParamDefValue &val) {
            llvm::SetVector<StringAttr> params;
            for (auto expr : val.exprs) {
              expr.walk([&](KGEN::ParamDeclRefAttr ref) {
                params.insert(ref.getName());
              });
            }
            defs[decl.getName()] = op;
            uses[decl.getName()] = params;
          });
    });
  }

  llvm::SetVector<Operation *> sorted;
  llvm::SetVector<StringAttr> inserted;
  llvm::SmallVector<StringAttr> workQueue;
  for (auto [k, _] : uses)
    workQueue.push_back(k);
  while (!workQueue.empty()) {
    StringAttr param = workQueue.back();
    workQueue.pop_back();

    if (inserted.contains(param))
      continue;
    auto notHandled =
        llvm::make_filter_range(uses.at(param), [&](StringAttr param) {
          return !inserted.contains(param);
        });
    if (notHandled.empty()) {
      inserted.insert(param);
      sorted.insert(defs[param]);
      continue;
    }

    workQueue.push_back(param);
    for (auto dependentParam : notHandled)
      workQueue.push_back(dependentParam);
  }

  for (auto op : ops)
    sorted.insert(op);
  return sorted.takeVector();
}

/// Returns false if `op` (an MO or KGEN param op) is definitely not constant
/// foldable.
static bool isMaybeConstantFoldable(Operation *op) {
  if (op->getNumRegions() != 0)
    return false;

  if (isa<MO::TransferOp>(op))
    return false;

  if (isa<MO::ConstantExternalOp>(op))
    return false;

  // Skip ops with parameterized shapes.
  auto findParamDeclRefAttr = [](KGEN::ParamDeclRefAttr paramRef) {
    return WalkResult::interrupt();
  };

  for (auto operandTy : op->getOperandTypes()) {
    if (operandTy.walk(findParamDeclRefAttr).wasInterrupted()) {
      return false;
    }
  }
  for (auto resultTy : op->getResultTypes()) {
    if (resultTy.walk(findParamDeclRefAttr).wasInterrupted()) {
      return false;
    }
  }
  for (auto attr : op->getAttrs()) {
    if (attr.getValue().walk(findParamDeclRefAttr).wasInterrupted()) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  // Skip ops which declare/define parameters (NOTE: they may not use them)
  auto paramOpInterface = dyn_cast<KGEN::ParamOpInterface>(op);
  if (paramOpInterface) {
    bool definesOrDeclaresParams = false;
    paramOpInterface.walkDefinitions(
        [&](KGEN::ParamDeclAttr param, const ParamDefValue &val) {
          definesOrDeclaresParams = true;
        });
    paramOpInterface.walkDeclarations(
        [&](KGEN::ParamDeclAttr param) { definesOrDeclaresParams = true; });
    if (definesOrDeclaresParams)
      return false;
  }

  bool isMOOrMOGG =
      isa_and_nonnull<MO::MODialect, MOGG::MOGGDialect>(op->getDialect());

  bool isSupportedMOOrMOGG =
      isMOOrMOGG &&
      // TODO (#29484): Graphs with mo.list outputs can't be lowered to
      // MGP.
      llvm::all_of(op->getResultTypes(), [](Type ty) {
        return isa<MO::TensorType, MOSH::ShapeType, MO::ListType>(ty);
      });

  bool isNonMOOrMOGGParamOp =
      isa<KGEN::ParamOpInterface, MOSH::ParamToValueOp, KGEN::ParamDeclareOp>(
          op) &&
      !isMOOrMOGG;

  return (isSupportedMOOrMOGG || isNonMOOrMOGGParamOp) &&
         !isa<MOControlOpInterface>(op);
}

/// Wraps a `dense_resource` as a tensor that owns the blob when all of the
/// constant's users are in `subgraph`, and borrows it otherwise.
static Tensor denseResourceAsTensor(AsyncRT::CPUDevice &cpuDevice,
                                    ConstantOp constant,
                                    DenseResourceElementsAttr denseResource,
                                    const Subgraph &subgraph) {
  mlir::AsmResourceBlob *blob = denseResource.getRawHandle().getBlob();
  TensorType ttype = dyn_cast<TensorType>(constant.getType());
  TensorSpec spec(ttype.getShape().getIntDims(), ttype.getDtype());

  llvm::SetVector<Operation *> constantUsers;
  for (Operation *user : constant->getUsers())
    constantUsers.insert(user);

  llvm::SetVector<Operation *> usersOutsideSubgraph(constantUsers);
  usersOutsideSubgraph.set_subtract(subgraph.ops);

  // Frees the blob when the buffer dies instead of with the MLIRContext.
  if (usersOutsideSubgraph.empty() && blob->getDeleter()) {
    auto async = AsyncValue::createReady<GML::BlobLifetimeManager>(
        cpuDevice.getCompactPtr(), std::move(blob->getDeleter()),
        blob->getData(), blob->getDataAlignment());
    blob->getDeleter() = nullptr;
    auto ref = AnyAsyncValueRef::take(async);

    TensorBufferRef blobBuffer = TensorBufferRef::create(
        reinterpret_cast<void *>(const_cast<char *>(blob->getData().data())),
        blob->getData().size(), std::move(ref), blob->getDataAlignment());
    return Tensor(std::move(blobBuffer), std::move(spec));
  }

  return Tensor::createBorrowed(blob->getData().data(), spec,
                                blob->getDataAlignment());
}

/// Creates an `ElementsAttr` with the same data as the tensor in `ref`.
static ElementsAttr elementsAttrFromTensor(const AnyAsyncValueRef &ref,
                                           MLIRContext *ctx) {
  // The size is only known at runtime.
  Tensor &tensor = ref.get<Tensor>();
  TensorShape shape = tensor.getShape();
  size_t numElements = shape.getNumElements();
  ArrayType arrayType = ArrayType::get(
      ctx, numElements, toMLIRType(ctx, tensor.getEltType()).get());
  ArrayRef<char> tensorData(tensor.getBuffer<char>(), tensor.getSizeInBytes());
  size_t elementByteAlign = llvm::PowerOf2Ceil(
      llvm::divideCeil(arrayType.getElementTypeBitWidth(), CHAR_BIT));
  if (!shouldUseOutOfLineAttrStorage(arrayType.getNumElements()))
    return getInlineAttrForTensorDataCopy(arrayType, tensorData);

  auto resourceManager = DenseResourceElementsHandle::getManagerInterface(ctx);
  TensorBufferRef &bufferRef = tensor.getBufferRef();

  // Reuses the data within the BufferRef and extends the lifetime of it.
  mlir::AsmResourceBlob blob =
      mlir::UnmanagedAsmResourceBlob::allocateWithAlign(
          tensorData, elementByteAlign,
          [storage = bufferRef.getMemStorageHandle().copy()](void *, size_t,
                                                             size_t) {
            // No-op that drops the `bufferRef` when the closure is
            // destroyed.
          });

  return DenseResourceElementsAttr::get(
      arrayType, resourceManager.insert("folded_const", std::move(blob)));
}

static RuntimeTensor extractRuntimeOutput(
    const AnyAsyncValueRef &ref, MLIRContext *ctx,
    llvm::DenseMap<M::AsyncRT::AsyncValue *, ElementsAttr> &cachedConstants) {
  if (M::MLRT::Tensor *tensor = dyn_cast<Tensor>(ref)) {
    ElementsAttr constantsArr;

    auto [it, inserted] =
        cachedConstants.try_emplace(ref.getPointer(), nullptr);
    if (!inserted) {
      // Reuse the attribute for a value returned more than once, avoiding
      // duplicate dense_resources. For example, folding:
      //  mo.if(...) {
      //     %1 = some_op(%cst0)
      //  } else {
      //     %1 = some_op(%cst0)
      // }
      // would otherwise generate:
      // mo.if(...) {
      //   %1 = constant { value=<folded_constant_1> }
      // } else {
      //   %1 = constant { value=<folded_constant_2> }
      // }
      constantsArr = it->second;
    } else {
      constantsArr = elementsAttrFromTensor(ref, ctx);
      it->second = constantsArr;
    }
    return RuntimeTensor(constantsArr, tensor->getShape());
  } else {
    ASSERT_STREAM(false, << "Unexpected GML type");
    llvm_unreachable("Unexpected GML type");
  }
}

/// Executes `model` and returns the folded constants and parameters of its
/// subgraphs.
static AsyncRT::ErrorDiagnosticOr<ModelResult>
executeModel(MLIRContext *ctx, const Model *model,
             const SubgraphsInfo &subgraphsInfo) {
  AsyncRT::CPUDevice &cpuDevice = *loadContext(ctx)->get<AsyncRT::CPUDevice>();
  auto executableModelOr = model->setup(cpuDevice);
  if (executableModelOr.isError())
    return executableModelOr.takeError();

  auto executableModel = executableModelOr.takeValue();
  auto setupErrorOrSuccess = executableModel->waitForModelSetup();
  if (setupErrorOrSuccess.isError()) {
    Error error("Model setup failed, not ready for execution"
                ".  Modular internal detail: " +
                Twine(setupErrorOrSuccess.getError()));
    return AsyncRT::EncodedDiagnostic(std::move(error),
                                      model->getLocation().copy());
  }

  SmallVector<AnyAsyncValueRef> inputTensorsInMEFIndexOrder;
  for (const Subgraph &subgraph : subgraphsInfo.subgraphs) {
    for (ConstantOp constant : subgraph.constants()) {
      if (auto denseResource =
              dyn_cast<DenseResourceElementsAttr>(constant.getValueAttr())) {
        inputTensorsInMEFIndexOrder.push_back(
            AsyncValueRef<Tensor>::createReady(
                cpuDevice, denseResourceAsTensor(cpuDevice, constant,
                                                 denseResource, subgraph)));
      }
    }
  }
  auto results = executableModel->execute(inputTensorsInMEFIndexOrder);

  if (results.empty())
    return AsyncRT::EncodedDiagnostic("Failed to execute model",
                                      model->getLocation().copy());

  AsyncRT::await(results);
  for (auto &result : results)
    if (result.isError())
      return AsyncRT::EncodedDiagnostic(result.takeDiagnostic());

  MutableArrayRef<AnyAsyncValueRef> resultCopy(results.data(), results.size());

  [[maybe_unused]] const AnyAsyncValueRef &ch = resultCopy.front();
  ASSERT_STREAM(ch.isReady() && isa<Chain>(ch),
                << "first result must be a ready chain");
  resultCopy = resultCopy.drop_front();

  llvm::DenseMap<M::AsyncRT::AsyncValue *, ElementsAttr> cachedConstants;
  llvm::DenseMap<Operation *, SmallVector<RuntimeTensor>> opToTensors;
  auto resourceManager = DenseResourceElementsHandle::getManagerInterface(ctx);
  size_t outIdx = 0;
  for (const Subgraph &subgraph : subgraphsInfo.subgraphs) {
    for (auto op : subgraph.outputs) {
      SmallVector<RuntimeTensor> opTensors;
      for (size_t i = 0; i < op->getNumResults(); ++i) {
        AnyAsyncValueRef &tensorRef = resultCopy[outIdx];
        if (tensorRef.isError())
          return AsyncRT::EncodedDiagnostic(tensorRef.takeDiagnostic());

        ASSERT_STREAM(tensorRef.isReady(),
                      << "We already awaited, tensor should be ready");
        opTensors.push_back(
            extractRuntimeOutput(tensorRef, ctx, cachedConstants));
        ++outIdx;
      }
      opToTensors[op] = opTensors;
    }
  }

  // Parameters follow the constants.
  llvm::DenseMap<StringAttr, int64_t> params;
  for (const Subgraph &subgraph : subgraphsInfo.subgraphs) {
    for (auto paramName : subgraph.paramDeclarations()) {
      auto &tensor = resultCopy[outIdx];
      if (tensor.isError())
        return AsyncRT::EncodedDiagnostic(tensor.takeDiagnostic());
      ASSERT_STREAM(tensor.isReady(),
                    << "We already awaited, tensor should be ready");
      ASSERT_STREAM(tensor.isType<Tensor>(),
                    << "Subgraph shape output should be a tensor");
      Tensor &t = tensor.get<Tensor>();
      params[paramName] = t.getBuffer<const int64_t>()[0];
      ++outIdx;
    }
  }
  return ModelResult{opToTensors, params};
}

SmallVector<Subgraph>
M::MO::constantSubgraphs(MO::GraphOp graph,
                         KGEN::ParameterCollector::Analysis &paramCache) {
  // Grows constant subgraphs from mo.constant users: an op is constant when
  // all its inputs are, resolving unvisited inputs first. Tracks ops rather
  // than values so parameters are covered too.

  llvm::DenseSet<Operation *> nonConst;

  // Constant ops (mo.constant, or all inputs constant); each class is a
  // connected DAG.
  llvm::EquivalenceClasses<Operation *> constSubgraphs;
  // An op is re-pushed until its operands are resolved.
  llvm::SmallVector<Operation *> workQueue;
  graph.walk([&](ConstantOp op) {
    auto dense = dyn_cast<DenseResourceElementsAttr>(op.getValueAttr());
    // Skip a dense_resource with no blob behind it.
    if (dense && !(dense.getRawHandle().getBlob())) {
      nonConst.insert(op);
      return;
    }
    constSubgraphs.insert(op);
    for (auto user : op.getResult().getUsers())
      workQueue.push_back(user);
  });

  // Bounded to catch unexpected infinite recursion.
  size_t iter = 0;
  size_t boundForMaximumIterations = 1000000;
  for (; iter < boundForMaximumIterations; ++iter) {
    if (workQueue.empty())
      break;

    Operation *op = workQueue.back();
    workQueue.pop_back();

    if (constSubgraphs.contains(op))
      continue;

    if (nonConst.contains(op))
      continue;

    if (!isMaybeConstantFoldable(op)) {
      nonConst.insert(op);
      continue;
    }

    llvm::DenseSet<Operation *> unvisitedOperands;
    bool foundNonConst = false;
    for (auto operand : op->getOperands()) {
      Operation *definition = operand.getDefiningOp();
      if (!definition || nonConst.contains(definition)) {
        foundNonConst = true;
        break;
      }
      if (!constSubgraphs.contains(definition)) {
        unvisitedOperands.insert(definition);
      }
    }
    if (foundNonConst) {
      nonConst.insert(op);
      continue;
    }

    // Revisit this op after its unvisited operands.
    if (!unvisitedOperands.empty()) {
      workQueue.push_back(op);
      for (auto definition : unvisitedOperands)
        workQueue.push_back(definition);
      continue;
    }

    constSubgraphs.insert(op);
    for (auto operand : op->getOperands())
      constSubgraphs.unionSets(op, operand.getDefiningOp());

    for (auto result : op->getResults())
      for (auto user : result.getUsers())
        workQueue.push_back(user);
  }
  ASSERT_STREAM(
      iter < boundForMaximumIterations,
      << "Hit bound on maximum number of iterations for detecting constant "
         "subgraphs. Either this graph op is VERY large (in which case this "
         "bound should be increased), or else somehow there is infinite "
         "recursion (which is a bug)");

  SmallVector<Subgraph> subgraphs;
  for (auto it = constSubgraphs.begin(), end = constSubgraphs.end(); it != end;
       ++it) {
    if (!(*it)->isLeader())
      continue;

    llvm::SetVector<Operation *> members(constSubgraphs.member_begin(*(*it)),
                                         constSubgraphs.member_end());
    Subgraph subgraph(members);

    // Ignore subgraphs that only contain constant ops.
    if (llvm::all_of(subgraph.ops, [](Operation *op) {
          return isa<ConstantOp>(op) || isa<KGEN::ParamDeclareOp>(op);
        }))
      continue;

    subgraphs.push_back(subgraph);
  }

  return subgraphs;
}

/// Clones `subgraphs` into a new GraphOp in `module` that outputs their
/// results. Large constants become inputs of the new graph.
static SubgraphsInfo cloneSubgraphs(OpBuilder builder, GraphOp orig,
                                    ModuleOp module,
                                    const SmallVector<Subgraph> &subgraphs,
                                    int64_t &name_counter) {
  llvm::SmallVector<Type> argTypes;
  for (const Subgraph &subgraph : subgraphs) {
    for (ConstantOp constant : subgraph.constants()) {
      auto dense = dyn_cast<DenseResourceElementsAttr>(constant.getValueAttr());
      if (dense)
        argTypes.push_back(constant.getType());
    }
  }
  llvm::SmallVector<Type> resultTypes;
  for (const Subgraph &subgraph : subgraphs) {
    for (auto op : subgraph.outputs) {
      for (auto result : op->getResults()) {
        resultTypes.push_back(result.getType());
      }
    }
  }

  // This name cannot be too long otherwise something breaks :(.
  auto graphop = GraphOp::create(builder, module.getLoc(),
                                 (orig.getName() + "_constant_subgraphs").str(),
                                 argTypes, resultTypes);
  builder.setInsertionPointToEnd(graphop.getBodyBlock());

  llvm::MapVector<ConstantOp, BlockArgument> inputConstants;
  int64_t idx = 0;
  for (const Subgraph &subgraph : subgraphs) {
    for (ConstantOp constant : subgraph.constants()) {
      auto dense = dyn_cast<DenseResourceElementsAttr>(constant.getValueAttr());
      if (dense) {
        inputConstants[constant] = graphop.getBody().getArgument(idx);
        ++idx;
      }
    }
  }

  SmallVector<Operation *, 8> opsSorted;
  for (const Subgraph &subgraph : subgraphs)
    for (auto op : subgraph.ops)
      opsSorted.push_back(op);
  // Param sorting alone doesn't keep definitions before uses.
  MLIRContext *ctx = module.getContext();
  Location loc = module.getLoc();
  opsSorted = sortParams(opsSorted);
  mlir::computeTopologicalSorting(opsSorted);
  IRMapping oldToNew;
  for (auto oldOp : opsSorted) {
    ConstantOp oldConst = dyn_cast<ConstantOp>(oldOp);
    if (oldConst) {
      auto it = inputConstants.find(oldConst);
      if (it != inputConstants.end()) {
        oldToNew.map(oldOp->getResult(0), it->second);
        continue;
      }
    }
    builder.clone(*oldOp, oldToNew);
  }
  llvm::SmallVector<Value> newOutputs;
  llvm::SetVector<KGEN::ParamDeclAttr> outputParams;
  for (const Subgraph &subgraph : subgraphs) {
    for (auto op : subgraph.outputs) {
      for (auto result : op->getResults()) {
        Value newOutput = oldToNew.lookup<Value>(result);
        if (llvm::isa<MOSH::ShapeType>(newOutput.getType())) {
          // Shapes can't be outputs; emit a rank-1 tensor of unknown size.
          newOutput = Shape::ToTensorOp::create(
              builder, loc,
              TensorType::get(
                  MOSH::ShapeAttr::get(
                      SmallVector<TypedAttr>(
                          {KGEN::UnknownAttr::get(builder.getIndexType())}),
                      MOSH::ShapeType::get(ctx)),
                  DType::si64),
              newOutput);
        }
        newOutput.getType().walk([&](KGEN::ParamDeclRefAttr ref) {
          outputParams.insert(KGEN::ParamDeclAttr::get(ref));
        });
        newOutputs.push_back(newOutput);
      }
    }
  }

  // Expose each subgraph parameter as a rank-0 int tensor output.
  Type dimTy = MOSH::ShapeAttr::getDimType(ctx);
  for (const Subgraph &subgraph : subgraphs) {
    for (auto param : subgraph.paramDeclarations()) {
      Value dimVal = MOSH::ParamToValueOp::create(
          builder, loc, KGEN::ParamDeclRefAttr::get(ctx, param, dimTy));
      newOutputs.push_back(IndexToTensorOp::create(
          builder, loc,
          TensorType::get(ArrayRef<int64_t>({}),
                          IntegerType::get(ctx, 64, IntegerType::Unsigned)),
          dimVal));
    }
  }
  // No output parameters because we bind them all locally.
  auto out = OutputOp::create(builder, loc, newOutputs,
                              KGEN::ParameterExprArrayAttr{});
  auto functionType =
      mlir::FunctionType::get(ctx, argTypes, out.getOperandTypes());
  graphop.setSignature(
      FuncTypeGeneratorType::get(/*inputParamTypes=*/{}, functionType));
  graphop.setFunctionType(functionType);

  // Rewrite all device attrs to target CPU.
  mlir::AttrTypeReplacer replacer;
  replacer.addReplacement(
      [&](DeviceRefAttr ref) { return DeviceRefAttr::get(ctx, kCPULabel, 0); });
  replacer.recursivelyReplaceElementsIn(graphop, /*replaceAttrs=*/true,
                                        /*replaceLocs=*/true,
                                        /*replaceTypes=*/true);

  return {orig, std::string(graphop.getName()), subgraphs};
}

static Value runtimeTensorToConstant(OpBuilder &builder, Location loc,
                                     RuntimeTensor tensor, Type type) {
  auto [constant, shape] = std::move(tensor);
  return ConstantOp::create(builder, loc, type, constant);
}

/// Replaces subgraph outputs with the executed constants, erases internal ops,
/// and declares the parameters the subgraph defined.
static void replaceConstantOps(GraphOp graph, OpBuilder &builder,
                               const ModelResult &modelResult,
                               const SubgraphsInfo &subgraphsInfo) {
  MLIRContext *ctx = graph.getContext();
  // Collect ops unused outside the subgraph before anything is erased.
  SmallVector<Operation *> allOps;
  for (const Subgraph &subgraph : subgraphsInfo.subgraphs)
    for (auto op : subgraph.ops)
      allOps.push_back(op);
  auto filtered = llvm::make_filter_range(allOps, [&](Operation *op) {
    return llvm::all_of(op->getResults(), [&](OpResult r) {
      return llvm::all_of(
          subgraphsInfo.subgraphs,
          [&](const Subgraph &subgraph) { return subgraph.fullyContains(r); });
    });
  });
  llvm::SmallVector<Operation *> internalOps(filtered.begin(), filtered.end());
  mlir::computeTopologicalSorting(internalOps);

  for (auto constant : modelResult.constants) {
    auto &op = constant.first;
    auto &tensors = constant.second;
    builder.setInsertionPoint(op);
    llvm::SmallVector<Value> newResults;
    for (auto [opResult, constantTensor] :
         llvm::zip(op->getResults(), tensors)) {
      Type type = opResult.getType();
      if (llvm::isa<TensorType>(type)) {
        newResults.push_back(runtimeTensorToConstant(builder, op->getLoc(),
                                                     constantTensor, type));
      } else if (llvm::isa<ShapeType>(type)) {
        auto intElems = dyn_cast<IntArrayElementsAttr>(constantTensor.first);
        newResults.push_back(MOSH::ParamToValueOp::create(
            builder, op->getLoc(),
            ShapeAttr::get(intElems.asArrayRef<int64_t>(),
                           MOSH::ShapeType::get(ctx))));
      } else {
        ASSERT_STREAM(false,
                      << "constants must be tensors or shapes but one is a "
                      << opResult.getType());
      }
    }
    op->replaceAllUsesWith(newResults);
    op->erase();
  }
  // Erase users before their operands.
  for (auto op : llvm::reverse(internalOps))
    op->erase();

  Type dimTy = MOSH::ShapeAttr::getDimType(ctx);
  builder.setInsertionPointToStart(graph.getBodyBlock());
  for (auto [name, val] : modelResult.parameters) {
    KGEN::ParamDeclareOp::create(builder, graph.getLoc(),
                                 KGEN::ParamDeclAttr::get(ctx, name, dimTy),
                                 MOSH::ShapeAttr::getDim(ctx, val));
  }
}

namespace M::MO {

struct ConstantFoldPass
    : public M::MO::impl::ConstantFoldBase<ConstantFoldPass> {
  using ConstantFoldBase::ConstantFoldBase;

  void runOnOperation() override;

  ConstantFoldPass()
      : ConstantFoldBase(), policy(new FactorMemIncrease(2, 100)) {}

  ConstantFoldPass(std::string_view label,
                   std::optional<std::string> cacheDirArg,
                   llvm::ArrayRef<std::string> kernelLibraryPathsArg)
      : ConstantFoldBase(), policy(new FactorMemIncrease(2, 100)),
        compiledFrameworkLabel(label), cacheDir(std::move(cacheDirArg)),
        kernelLibraryPaths(kernelLibraryPathsArg.vec()) {}

private:
  // Lowers `module` to MGP and MEF, then loads its models along with the
  // buffer that must outlive them. A member so it can call runPipeline.
  ErrorOr<ModelsWithBuffer> lowerSubgraphs(ModuleOp module);

  std::shared_ptr<const ConstantFoldPolicy> policy;
  // Label used to load and handle buffers created by the framework.
  std::string compiledFrameworkLabel;
  std::optional<std::string> cacheDir;
  std::optional<std::vector<std::string>> kernelLibraryPaths;
};

ErrorOr<ModelsWithBuffer> ConstantFoldPass::lowerSubgraphs(ModuleOp module) {
  // Seed the sub-module with the parent's kernel decls so the sub-pipeline
  // skips re-importing the `kernels` / `primitives` packages. A standalone
  // `mo-opt` run has no decls, so the sub-pipeline imports them itself.
  bool parentHasDecls =
      (*getOperation()).hasAttr(MOGG::kOpaqueTypeMappingAttrName);
  if (parentHasDecls)
    MOGG::copyKernelDecls(getOperation(), module);

  mlir::PassManager pm = mlir::PassManager::on<ModuleOp>(
      module.getContext(), mlir::OpPassManager::Nesting::Implicit);
  MO::GCPipelineOptions moOpts;
  if (cacheDir)
    moOpts.cacheDir = cacheDir.value();
  if (kernelLibraryPaths)
    moOpts.kernelLibraryPaths = kernelLibraryPaths.value();
  // `copyKernelDecls` already provided the decls and opaque-type mapping.
  moOpts.kernelDeclsPrePopulated = parentHasDecls;
  // Avoid infinite recursion.
  moOpts.constantFold = false;
  // Reduce Memory overhead by not fusing compiled-regions primitives.
  moOpts.enableCompiledRegionsFusion = false;
  // Use MOF executor so we don't depend on a framework.
  moOpts.compiledFrameworkLabel = "mof";
  // This will fold sequence of trivial kernels not on host, leading to huge
  // kernels (in terms of code size).
  moOpts.performTrivialKernelOpt = false;
  // Assign ops to CPU so we can constant fold there.
  moOpts.defaultDeviceLabel = kCPULabel;
  // The folded model runs in this process, so compile for the host even when
  // a virtual CPU target retargets the outer compile (e.g. a Windows-ABI
  // object fails JIT-linking `__chkstk`).
  moOpts.compileForHostExecution = true;
  // Force all kernels to be synchronous. This prevents higher memory usage.
  moOpts.forceSync = true;
  // Constant subgraphs are mostly isolated, so post-ABI fusion only costs
  // compile time.
  moOpts.enablePostABIRegionFusion = false;
  MO::buildGCPipeline(pm, moOpts);
  if (failed(runPipeline(pm, module)))
    return Error("Failed to run MOToMGP pipeline");

  // `convertMGPToMEF` destroys `dense_resource`s; this is safe only because
  // folded subgraphs take them as operands, not attributes.
  auto bufOr = MO::convertMGPToMEF(module, false);
  if (bufOr)
    return bufOr.takeError();
  BufferRef buf = bufOr.takeValue();

  auto mefFileOr = MEFFile::open(buf->getBuffer());
  if (mefFileOr.isError())
    return mefFileOr.takeError();
  auto modelsOr = M::loadMGPMEFFile(std::move(*mefFileOr));
  if (modelsOr.isError())
    return modelsOr.takeError();
  return {ModelsWithBuffer{modelsOr.takeValue(), std::move(buf)}};
}

void ConstantFoldPass::runOnOperation() {
  MLIRContext *ctx = &getContext();

  for (auto _ : llvm::seq(iterations.getValue())) {
    OpBuilder builder(ctx);
    builder.setInsertionPointToStart(getOperation().getBody());
    // Passes can't create ops outside their operation, so subgraph copies
    // live in a temporary nested module.
    OwningOpRef<ModuleOp> module = ModuleOp::create(
        builder, getOperation().getLoc(), "constant_fold_subgraphs");

    // Alternative: merge all subgraphs into one graph and execute it once.
    SmallVector<SubgraphsInfo> subgraphReplacements;
    int64_t nameCounter = 0;

    for (auto graph : getOperation().getOps<GraphOp>()) {
      auto subgraphs = M::MO::constantSubgraphs(
          graph, getAnalysis<KGEN::ParameterCollector::Analysis>());
      if (subgraphs.empty())
        continue;

      subgraphs = policy->shouldFold(subgraphs);
      if (subgraphs.empty())
        continue;

      builder.setInsertionPointToEnd(module->getBody());
      subgraphReplacements.push_back(
          cloneSubgraphs(builder, graph, *module, subgraphs, nameCounter));
    }

    if (module->getBody()->getOperations().empty()) {
      // Remove module to not clutter the output.
      continue;
    }

    ErrorOr<ModelsWithBuffer> modelsOr = lowerSubgraphs(*module);
    if (failed(modelsOr)) {
      mlir::emitError(module->getLoc(),
                      "[ConstantFold] Unable to load models: " +
                          Twine(modelsOr.getError()));
      return signalPassFailure();
    }

    // We have to consolidate rewrites to the same graphop as the rewrites of
    // one subgraph may invalidate ops in another subgraph (due to fold
    // hooks).
    llvm::DenseMap<GraphOp,
                   llvm::SmallVector<std::pair<ModelResult, SubgraphsInfo>>>
        executionResults;
    for (const SubgraphsInfo &subgraphsInfo : subgraphReplacements) {
      const Model *model = findModelByName(modelsOr.get().models,
                                           subgraphsInfo.constantSubgraphName);
      ASSERT_STREAM(model != nullptr,
                    << "Model should exist, make sure names above match.");
      AsyncRT::ErrorDiagnosticOr<ModelResult> valuesToTensorsOr =
          executeModel(ctx, model, subgraphsInfo);
      if (valuesToTensorsOr.isError()) {
        AsyncRT::EncodedDiagnostic err = valuesToTensorsOr.takeError();
        auto decoded = err.decodeLocation();
        auto loc =
            mlir::FileLineColLoc::get(module->getContext(), decoded.filename,
                                      decoded.line, decoded.column);
        mlir::emitError(loc, "[ConstantFold] " + Twine(err.getMessage().get()));
        return signalPassFailure();
      }

      ModelResult valuesToTensors = valuesToTensorsOr.takeValue();

      auto [it, _] = executionResults.try_emplace(
          subgraphsInfo.orig,
          SmallVector<std::pair<ModelResult, SubgraphsInfo>>());

      it->second.push_back({std::move(valuesToTensors), subgraphsInfo});
    }

    // Replace output expressions with constants. Also handles DCE.
    for (auto [graph, results] : executionResults) {
      for (const auto &r : results) {
        const ModelResult &modelResult = r.first;
        const SubgraphsInfo &subgraphsInfo = r.second;
        replaceConstantOps(graph, builder, modelResult, subgraphsInfo);
      }
    }

    // Remove module to not clutter the output.
    module = nullptr;

    // Run canonicalization to uncover more constant folding.
    mlir::PassManager pm = mlir::PassManager::on<ModuleOp>(
        ctx, mlir::OpPassManager::Nesting::Implicit);
    pm.addNestedPass<GraphOp>(mlir::createCanonicalizerPass());
    if (failed(pm.run(getOperation()))) {
      mlir::emitError(getOperation().getLoc(),
                      "[ConstantFold] Failed to run canonicalization pass");
      return signalPassFailure();
    }
  }
}

} // namespace M::MO

std::unique_ptr<mlir::Pass>
M::MO::createConstantFoldPass(std::string_view compiledFrameworkLabel,
                              std::optional<std::string> cacheDir,
                              llvm::ArrayRef<std::string> kernelLibraryPaths) {
  return std::make_unique<ConstantFoldPass>(
      compiledFrameworkLabel, std::move(cacheDir), kernelLibraryPaths);
}
